ACCUMULATOR DRAIN ^GOyMP6q3

BIAS +
ACTIVATION ^BtkJuzh1

REQUANTIZE ^GSAEEhVE

WRITEBACK ^EnSWGQmm

COMPUTE CONTRACT  /  SYNTHETIC TEACHING TILE ^EWffBdz3

C[i,j] = sum_k A[i,k] × B[k,j]     |     16 MAC cells     |     2 × operand staging banks ^1Ykf4KwT

Reuse: one A stream serves four columns; one B stream serves four rows. Array fill/drain and memory service determine utilization. ^VBHq1cpS

Architectural illustration, not a chip performance claim. Per-column drain is schematic; timing, precision, arbitration and edge buffering are not simulated. ^2MXRbfl8

Purple C lanes are used only in the drain phase; each PE retains its own accumulator during the MAC phase. ^41pY7xwo

    {
      "text": "COMPUTE CONTRACT  /  SYNTHETIC TEACHING TILE",

      "originalText": "COMPUTE CONTRACT  /  SYNTHETIC TEACHING TILE",
      "rawText": "COMPUTE CONTRACT  /  SYNTHETIC TEACHING TILE",

      "autoResize": true,
      "customData": {

      "index": "b2S"
    },
    {
      "text": "C[i,j] = sum_k A[i,k] × B[k,j]     |     16 MAC cells     |     2 × operand staging banks",
      "fontSize": 29,
      "fontFamily": "Liberation Sans",
      "textAlign": "left",
      "verticalAlign": "top",
      "id": "1Ykf4KwT",
      "type": "text",
      "x": 90,
      "y": 1680,
      "width": 1036.14111328125,
      "height": 34.8,
      "angle": 0,
      "strokeColor": "#17324d",
      "backgroundColor": "transparent",
      "fillStyle": "solid",
      "strokeWidth": 2,
      "strokeStyle": "solid",
      "roughness": 0,
      "opacity": 100,
      "roundness": null,
      "seed": 96411,
      "version": 2,
      "versionNonce": 1287090534,
      "updated": 1792161777453,
      "isDeleted": false,
      "groupIds": [],
      "boundElements": [],
      "link": null,
      "locked": false,
      "frameId": null,
      "hasTextLink": false,
      "created": 1792161777434,
      "containerId": null,
      "originalText": "C[i,j] = sum_k A[i,k] × B[k,j]     |     16 MAC cells     |     2 × operand staging banks",
      "rawText": "C[i,j] = sum_k A[i,k] × B[k,j]     |     16 MAC cells     |     2 × operand staging banks",
      "lineHeight": 1.2,
      "autoResize": true,
      "customData": {
        "agentDiagram": {
          "diagramId": "advanced-accelerator",
          "logicalId": "equation",
          "role": "text"
        }
      },
      "index": "b2T"
    },
    {
      "text": "Reuse: one A stream serves four columns; one B stream serves four rows. Array fill/drain and memory service determine utilization.",
      "fontSize": 23,
      "fontFamily": "Liberation Sans",
      "textAlign": "left",
      "verticalAlign": "top",
      "id": "VBHq1cpS",
      "type": "text",

      "y": 1740,

      "height": 27.599999999999998,
      "angle": 0,
      "strokeColor": "#0f766e",
      "backgroundColor": "transparent",
      "fillStyle": "solid",
      "strokeWidth": 2,
      "strokeStyle": "solid",
      "roughness": 0,
      "opacity": 100,
      "roundness": null,

      "frameId": null,
      "hasTextLink": false,
      "created": 1792161777435,
      "containerId": null,
      "originalText": "Reuse: one A stream serves four columns; one B stream serves four rows. Array fill/drain and memory service determine utilization.",
      "rawText": "Reuse: one A stream serves four columns; one B stream serves four rows. Array fill/drain and memory service determine utilization.",

    {
